# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

Read · measurement.md
# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 3 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high |

| dc_low | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_low |
| dc_low | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_low |
| dc_mid | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_mid |
| dc_mid | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_mid |
| dc_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_high |
| dc_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | dc_high |
| light_heavy | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | light_heavy |
| light_heavy | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | light_heavy |

| psrr_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | psrr_high |
| psrr_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 1 | psrr_high |

Defaults and seed requirements:

- nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_low: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_mid: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_high: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- light_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- mid_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- burst: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- line: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_low: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
- psrr_high: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.

MODEL
0:06:50

The first measurement shows the DC checks pass with margin, but the transient checks fail: droop is 121 to 254 mV against an 80 mV limit, the 80 mV overshoot limit is also exceeded on the falling edge, and the rising-edge recovery exceeds 5 µs. The 20 pF Miller cap multiplies the gate capacitance by the pass device's gain at light load, which slows the gate pole. I'll remove that cap and raise the error-amp tail current to speed up the loop.

0:06:52

content
* LDO v2: PMOS pass, NMOS-pair error amplifier with PMOS mirror load,
* mirrored NMOS tail from a resistor-biased diode, resistive divider, no Miller cap.

XMN2 EAO VREF TL VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1

.ends DUT

0:07:18

The overhead dropped to 8.8 µA, which shows the m=8 multiplier was dropped in normalization and the tail stayed at about 7.5 µA, so droop worsened to 477 mV. I'll replace the multiplier with a wider tail device (W=80 µm, L=1 µm) for about 8× mirror ratio, which targets roughly 60 µA tail and about 110 kΩ error-amp output resistance.

MODEL
0:10:23

The measured tail is still about 8 µA, so the wider tail device did not help. The real cause is headroom: with the 0.5 V reference, the NMOS input pair's source sits near ground and the tail transistor drops out of saturation. I'll shift the error-amp inputs up with PMOS source followers biased through resistors, which lifts the common mode to about 1.05 V. Then I'll test three variants in one batch: no Miller cap at 37 µA tail, 5 pF Miller at 37 µA, and no Miller at about 19 µA tail.

MODEL
0:17:09

All three variants have DC error about 0.25 V, so the error amp is not regulating even though the tail now conducts (31 µA overhead). The likely cause is headroom in the PMOS mirror load: its gate-source voltage (about 0.85 V at 15 µA with W=10, L=1) pushes the diode-side drain down to roughly the tail voltage, leaving the diode-side input device in triode. I'll widen the mirror to W=40 to cut its overdrive, and test that with and without a 5 pF Miller cap plus a half-current tail.

Widening the mirror did not fix the 0.25 V DC error, and it shows the real limit: the NMOS-input stack needs about 1.1 V at its gate, which leaves no headroom at VIN = 1.15 V. I'll switch to a PMOS input pair driven directly by VREF and FB at 0.5 V, with a PMOS tail from VIN and an NMOS mirror load. That needs no level shift. Testing three sizings of that topology at nominal N.

The PMOS-input amp now regulates: DC error is 21 mV at 25 µA but 63–102 mV at heavier loads, and the falling-edge recovery is fast. The transient envelope (about 0.10 V) still fails the 80 mV limit, and the rising edge never recovers. The tail draws only about 10 µA, which is too slow for the gate node, so I'll try three bias settings: a 20k bias resistor, the same with a half-size pass device, and a 10k bias resistor.

0:23:35

Bias changes barely moved the tail current (overhead still 10–17 µA), so the tail is headroom-limited at this input length. The DC error at heavy load appears to come from the input pair's drain-voltage mismatch, which grows as the input channel shortens. Intermediate input lengths should trade DC accuracy against transient speed, so I'll test L=0.2 and L=0.25, plus variant n with a 33k bias.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |
| dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 3 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high |

Defaults and seed requirements:

Write · sweep.py
/tmp/sweep.py
content
import json, re, shutil, subprocess, sys

TEMPLATE = """.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
{passes}XPA D1 FB TP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wi} L={li} nf=1 m=1
XPB EAO VREF TP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wi} L={li} nf=1 m=1
XNA D1 D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wn} L={ln} nf=1 m=1
XNB EAO D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wn} L={ln} nf=1 m=1
XPT TP VBP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wt} L={lt} nf=1 m=1
XPD VBP VBP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wd} L={ld} nf=1 m=1
RBP VBP VSS {rbp}
R1 VOUT FB 500k
R2 FB VSS 500k
.ends DUT
"""

BASE = dict(n=3, wi=20, li=0.15, wn=10, ln=1, wt=100, lt=0.15, wd=10, ld=0.15, rbp="100k")

VARS = {
    "e1": dict(rbp="33k"),
    "e2": dict(rbp="10k"),
    "e3": dict(rbp="4.7k"),
    "e4": dict(n=1),
    "e5": dict(n=1, rbp="33k"),
    "e6": dict(wn=20),
    "e7": dict(wi=40),
    "e8": dict(wt=200, rbp="33k"),
}


def build(cfg):
    passes = "".join(
        f"XMP{i} VOUT EAO VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1\n"
        for i in range(cfg["n"]))
    return TEMPLATE.format(passes=passes, **{k: v for k, v in cfg.items() if k != "n"})


def measure(name, cfg):
    vpath = f"/workspace/var_{name}.spice"
    with open(vpath, "w") as f:
        f.write(build(cfg))
    req = f"/tmp/req_{name}.json"
    with open(req, "w") as f:
        json.dump({"action": "measure", "experiment_id": f"v5{name}-nominal-N-2e10",
                   "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": "N", "cl": 2e-10}, f)
    shutil.copy(vpath, "/workspace/dut.spice")
    out = f"/tmp/m_v5{name}.json"
    with open(out, "w") as f:
        subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", req], stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    return subprocess.run(["python3", "/tmp/summ.py", out], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout


def fmt(v, scale, nd=1):
    return "-" if v == "None" else f"{float(v) * scale:.{nd}f}"


def compact(name, s):
    rows = {}
    oh = []
    miss = 0
    for line in s.splitlines():
        m = re.match(r"^(\S+)\s+(\S+)\s+(\S+)\s+lim\s+(\S+)\s+(PASS|MISS)", line)
        if not m:
            continue
        case, metric, val, _, status = m.groups()
        rows[(case, metric)] = val
        if status == "MISS":
            miss += 1
        if metric == "overhead_a":
            oh.append(float(val) * 1e6)
    rem = re.search(r"remaining (\S+) used (\S+)", s)
    g = lambda c, mt, sc, nd=1: fmt(rows.get((c, mt), "None"), sc, nd)
    return (f"{name:4s} dc {g('dc_low','dc_error_v',1e3)}/{g('dc_mid','dc_error_v',1e3)}/"
            f"{g('dc_high','dc_error_v',1e3)} mV  oh {max(oh):.1f} uA  | "
            f"LH env {g('light_heavy','envelope_error_v',1e3)} r0 {g('light_heavy','recovery_0_s',1e6,2)} "
            f"r1 {g('light_heavy','recovery_1_s',1e6,2)} | "
            f"MH env {g('mid_heavy','envelope_error_v',1e3)} r0 {g('mid_heavy','recovery_0_s',1e6,2)} "
            f"r1 {g('mid_heavy','recovery_1_s',1e6,2)} | "
            f"BU env {g('burst','envelope_error_v',1e3)} r0 {g('burst','recovery_0_s',1e6,2)} | "
            f"miss {miss} | rem {rem.group(1)[:7] if rem else '?'}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    names = sys.argv[1:] or list(VARS)
    for name in names:
        cfg = dict(BASE)
        cfg.update(VARS[name])
        print(compact(name, measure(name, cfg)), flush=True)

File created successfully at: /tmp/sweep.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

0:36:34
Write · inv.py
/tmp/inv.py
content
import json, shutil, subprocess, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp")
from sweep import compact

TEMPLATE = """.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
{passes}XPA D1 VREF TP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wi} L={li} nf=1 m=1
XPB EAO FB TP VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wi} L={li} nf=1 m=1
XNA D1 D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wn} L={ln} nf=1 m=1
XNB EAO D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wn} L={ln} nf=1 m=1
XPT TP VBP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
XPD VBP VBP VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
RBP VBP VSS {rbp}
XIP G EAO VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wip} L={lip} nf=1 m=1
XIN G EAO VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={win} L={lin} nf=1 m=1
R1 VOUT FB 500k
R2 FB VSS 500k
.ends DUT
"""

BASE = dict(n=3, wi=20, li=0.15, wn=20, ln=1, rbp="100k", wip=20, lip=0.5, win=10, lin=0.5)

VARS = {
    "q1": dict(),
    "q2": dict(n=1),
    "q3": dict(wip=40, win=20),
}


def build(cfg):
    passes = "".join(
        f"XMP{i} VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1\n"
        for i in range(cfg["n"]))
    return TEMPLATE.format(passes=passes, **{k: v for k, v in cfg.items() if k != "n"})


def run(name, cfg):
    vpath = f"/workspace/var_{name}.spice"
    with open(vpath, "w") as f:
        f.write(build(cfg))
    req = f"/tmp/req_{name}.json"
    with open(req, "w") as f:
        json.dump({"action": "measure", "experiment_id": f"v6{name}-nominal-N-2e10",
                   "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": "N", "cl": 2e-10}, f)
    shutil.copy(vpath, "/workspace/dut.spice")
    out = f"/tmp/m_v6{name}.json"
    with open(out, "w") as f:
        subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", req], stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    s = subprocess.run(["python3", "/tmp/summ.py", out], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    return compact(name, s) if "dc_low" in s else f"{name} FAILED: " + open(out).read()[:200]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    for name in sys.argv[1:] or list(VARS):
        cfg = dict(BASE)
        cfg.update(VARS[name])
        print(run(name, cfg), flush=True)

content
import json, shutil, subprocess, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp")
from sweep import compact

SRC = sys.argv[1]
TAG = sys.argv[2]
CASES = [("N", 2e-10), ("N", 5e-10), ("SLC", 2e-10), ("SLH", 2e-10), ("SHC", 2e-10),
         ("SHH", 2e-10), ("FLC", 2e-10), ("FLH", 2e-10), ("FHC", 2e-10), ("FHH", 2e-10)]

shutil.copy(SRC, "/workspace/dut.spice")
for pvt, cl in CASES:
    clt = f"{cl:.0e}".replace("e-10", "e10").replace("+", "")
    tag = f"{TAG}-{pvt}-{clt}"
    req = f"/tmp/req_{tag}.json"
    with open(req, "w") as f:
        json.dump({"action": "measure", "experiment_id": f"v7{tag}",
                   "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt, "cl": cl}, f)
    out = f"/tmp/m_v7{tag}.json"
    with open(out, "w") as f:
        subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", req], stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    s = subprocess.run(["python3", "/tmp/summ.py", out], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    print(compact(f"{pvt}/{clt}", s) if "dc_low" in s else f"{tag} FAILED", flush=True)

The candidate fails badly across corners (for example, SLC light-to-heavy envelope 426 mV, FHH heavy-load DC 177 mV), so it is not robust. The cause looks like the tail's saturation headroom: its overdrive is set by the bias chain (RBP and XPD), so it shifts with process and voltage. A higher RBP should lower the tail overdrive and widen the margin, so I'll check that at nominal first.

Bash

Tabulate prior nominal results with their netlist configs

cd /tmp && python3 - <<'EOF'
import glob, os, re, subprocess, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp")
from sweep import compact

def cfg(var):
    p = f"/workspace/var_{var}.spice"
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return "(no var file)"
    t = open(p).read()
    def g(tag, key):
        m = re.search(rf"^{tag} .*?{key}=(\S+)", t, re.M)
        return m.group(1) if m else "?"
    n = len(re.findall(r"^XMP\d+", t, re.M))
    rbp = re.search(r"^RBP .* (\S+)$", t, re.M)
    return (f"n{n} PA{g('XPA','W')}/{g('XPA','L')} PB{g('XPB','W')}/{g('XPB','L')} "
            f"NA{g('XNA','W')}/{g('XNA','L')} PT{g('XPT','W')}/{g('XPT','L')} "
            f"PD{g('XPD','W')}/{g('XPD','L')} rbp{rbp.group(1) if rbp else '?'}")

for f in sorted(glob.glob("/tmp/m_v[456]*.json")):
    name = os.path.basename(f)[2:-5]
    s = subprocess.run(["python3", "/tmp/summ.py", f], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    if "dc_low" not in s:
        continue
    var = re.sub(r"^v\d", "", name)
    print(compact(name, s))
    print("      ", cfg(var))
EOF

Screen candidate designs at SLC, FLH and FHH corners

cat > /tmp/screen.py <<'EOF'
import json, shutil, subprocess, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp")
from sweep import compact

CASES = [("SLC", 2e-10), ("FLH", 2e-10), ("FHH", 2e-10)]
for name in sys.argv[1:]:
    shutil.copy(f"/workspace/var_{name}.spice", "/workspace/dut.spice")
    for pvt, cl in CASES:
        tag = f"{name}-{pvt}"
        req = f"/tmp/req_v8{tag}.json"
        with open(req, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"action": "measure", "experiment_id": f"v8{tag}",
                       "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt, "cl": cl}, f)
        out = f"/tmp/m_v8{tag}.json"
        with open(out, "w") as f:
            subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", req], stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        s = subprocess.run(["python3", "/tmp/summ.py", out], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        print(compact(tag, s) if "dc_low" in s else f"{tag} FAILED", flush=True)
EOF

Submit the t5 design with rationale through the client

atb-client submit --rationale "$(cat <<'EOF'
Partial design. It does not meet the spec; the failures below are known and were measured.

Design: single-stage PMOS-input error amplifier with a 500k/500k divider (VOUT = 2*VFB). Pair: two PMOS W=20 um, L=0.15 um, gates FB and VREF, sources on tail node TP. Tail: PMOS W=100 um, L=0.15 um, mirrored from a diode-connected PMOS W=10 um, L=0.15 um, biased by RBP=50k to VSS. Load: NMOS diode mirror NA and mirror NB, W=60 um, L=1 um. Output EAO drives six parallel PMOS pass devices (W=100 um, L=0.15 um each, body on VIN). No capacitors; sum of W*L = 232.5 um^2. Chosen as the better of the two candidates run through the full matrix (67 failing checks, against 71 for the other), and it passes the FHH light-load overhead (78 uA against 100 uA).

Known failures:
- Nominal N at 200 pF: dc_high 39 mV against 30 mV; light_heavy and mid_heavy recovery_0 not met (no return to 0.97-1.03 V within 5 us). Nominal at 500 pF has three failing checks.
- All eight corners (SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH) have failing checks. The worst are SLC (light_heavy envelope about 429 mV against 80 mV, 10 failing checks) and FHH (14 failing checks, DC low about 184 mV).
- Across every L=0.15 um single-stage variant tested, dc_low plus dc_high at nominal is about 68-70 mV (inferred; the client reports magnitudes only). That is wider than the +/-30 mV window, so no bias point found passes both light-load and heavy-load DC.

Total: 67 failing checks across the ten measured cases (N at 200 and 500 pF, eight corners at 200 pF).
EOF
)"

MODEL
1:03:09

Submitted the t5 design as revision dut-1b76b39127ed55cc122965ab. It does not meet the spec, and the client returned no verification feedback after submission.

Design decisions:

  • Single-stage PMOS-input error amplifier. An NMOS-input pair had no headroom at VREF = 0.5 V, and a level-shifted version starved the diode side.
  • Tail PMOS (W=100, L=0.15) mirrors a diode PMOS through RBP = 50k. RBP sets the quiescent overhead, about 33 µA at nominal.
  • Six parallel PMOS pass devices (body on VIN) for drive. There are no capacitors, and gate area is 232.5 µm².
  • Chosen over t2 on the full corner matrix: 67 failing checks against 71. t2 also fails the FHH light-load overhead limit.

Known failures (67 failing checks across the 10 measured cases):

  • Nominal dc_high is 39 mV against 30 mV. dc_low plus dc_high is about 68 mV, which is wider than the ±30 mV window. This is inferred because the client reports magnitudes only. None of the variants I tried passed both light and heavy loads.
  • Light- and mid-heavy recovery_0 fail at nominal: the output does not return to 0.97–1.03 V within 5 µs.
  • All eight corners fail. SLC is the worst, with a load-step envelope of about 429 mV against 80 mV.

I deleted my 101 scratch var_*.spice files. dut.spice remains as the submitted netlist, and exploration is now closed.
